CRIMINAL CASE DIGITAL EVIDENCE INTEGRATED RECORD
Synthetic Long-Form Dataset for RAG Retrieval, Reranking, and Evidence-Grounded Answer Evaluation

Document Control No.: SYN-PROS-EVIDENCE-20CASE-EN-2026-001
Classification: Fully Synthetic Investigative Evidence Dataset

Purpose:
- Evaluate retrieval performance over long-form multi-case evidence records
- Measure Recall@K, nDCG@K, MRR, and Evidence Coverage
- Evaluate reranker performance and multi-evidence retrieval
- Evaluate citation accuracy, answer correctness, and evidence-grounded faithfulness

IMPORTANT NOTICE:
Every case, person, organization, bank account, telephone number, email address, IP address,
vehicle identifier, location, financial amount, and alleged criminal act in this document is fictitious.
The dataset was generated solely for RAG evaluation and is unrelated to any real person or real case.

DATA DESIGN PRINCIPLES:
1. The dataset emphasizes raw evidentiary records rather than narrative case summaries.
2. Core facts are deliberately distributed across different evidence types and different portions of the document.
3. Each case contains direct evidence, circumstantial evidence, neutral/background material, and irrelevant noise.
4. Questions should require temporal, financial, geographic, and interpersonal linkage rather than simple keyword matching.
5. Each case contains twenty evidence bundles, for a total of four hundred evidence bundles.
6. Evidence categories include messenger records, deleted-message recovery, email, call records, GPS,
   cell-site data, CCTV, banking, payment records, browser history, server logs, cloud activity,
   document versioning, social media, seized items, access control, and cross-evidence timelines.

# CASE 1
# International Methamphetamine Smuggling, Domestic Distribution, and Proceeds Concealment Case

Case No.: 2026-CR-4101
Suspected Offenses: narcotics smuggling, domestic distribution, concealment of criminal proceeds
Principal Persons: Doyun Kim, Taehoon Lee, Seojin Park, Wooseok Jung, Gyuhyun Choi
Relevant Organizations: Serim Trading, Dongjin Distribution, Fine Logistics, VN Parts Export
Principal Investigative Fact Pattern: approximately 3 kg of methamphetamine was concealed inside industrial bearing samples, imported through Southeast Port, divided at a warehouse, and transferred to domestic distributors
Principal Amount or Scale: USD 96,000 and KRW 32,000,000 in cash
Principal Evidence Object: methamphetamine, industrial bearing housings, cash proceeds
Principal Locations: Southeast Port Pier 4 · Dongjin Distribution Warehouse B · Nambu Station Parking Lot 2

This section is structured as an integrated body of seized and collected digital, financial, communications, location, video-metadata, enterprise-system, and physical-evidence records. It is not intended as a narrative case summary; the relevant facts are intentionally dispersed across multiple evidence bundles.

# CASE 2
# Hanbit City Development Bribery, Bid-Rigging, and Slush-Fund Case

Case No.: 2026-CR-4127
Suspected Offenses: bribery, bid-rigging, embezzlement, creation of off-book funds
Principal Persons: Taesung Kang, Yoonho Seo, Jinseok Oh, Jaemin Yoon, Sera Hong
Relevant Organizations: Cheongmyeong Construction, Daesung Civil Engineering, J1 Consulting, Hanbit City Development Division
Principal Investigative Fact Pattern: false consulting expenses were converted to cash and allegedly delivered to a public official in connection with development approval, while competing contractors coordinated bid prices in advance
Principal Amount or Scale: approximately KRW 100,000,000 in alleged bribes and KRW 80,000,000 in sham consulting fees
Principal Evidence Object: cash, sham consulting contracts, confidential bid information
Principal Locations: Hanbit City Hall · Cheongyeon Restaurant · Central Golf Practice Center

This section is structured as an integrated body of seized and collected digital, financial, communications, location, video-metadata, enterprise-system, and physical-evidence records. It is not intended as a narrative case summary; the relevant facts are intentionally dispersed across multiple evidence bundles.

# CASE 3
# River Capital AI Auto-Trading Investment Fraud and Misappropriation Case

Case No.: 2026-CR-4153
Suspected Offenses: large-scale fraud and misappropriation of investment funds
Principal Persons: Minho Jung, Yerin Kim, Seungchan Oh, Harin Moon, Jiho Park
Relevant Organizations: River Capital, River AI Fund, RM Mobility
Principal Investigative Fact Pattern: an investor dashboard with no live brokerage API connection was marketed as a functioning AI trading system, while new investor deposits were used to pay returns to earlier investors

Principal Evidence Object: investor funds and simulated trading dashboard
Principal Locations: River Capital Headquarters · Investor Seminar Hall · CEO Residence

This section is structured as an integrated body of seized and collected digital, financial, communications, location, video-metadata, enterprise-system, and physical-evidence records. It is not intended as a narrative case summary; the relevant facts are intentionally dispersed across multiple evidence bundles.

# CASE 4
# Dongsan Electronics Semiconductor Trade-Secret Exfiltration Case

Case No.: 2026-CR-4188
Suspected Offenses: misappropriation and unauthorized disclosure of trade secrets
Principal Persons: Hyunwoo Jo, Seyoung Park, Jihoon Ryu, Sora Han, Dongmin Choi
Relevant Organizations: Dongsan Electronics, NeoChip Solutions
Principal Investigative Fact Pattern: a departing semiconductor engineer accessed confidential design servers late at night, copied core design and yield-analysis files to a USB device, and later opened the files on a competitor workstation
Principal Amount or Scale: KRW 30,000,000 special technical contribution bonus
Principal Evidence Object: NX7 semiconductor design data and USB media
Principal Locations: Dongsan Electronics R&D Center · NeoChip Solutions Research Building

This section is structured as an integrated body of seized and collected digital, financial, communications, location, video-metadata, enterprise-system, and physical-evidence records. It is not intended as a narrative case summary; the relevant facts are intentionally dispersed across multiple evidence bundles.

# CASE 6
# Saebom Hospital False-Admission Insurance Fraud Case

Case No.: 2026-CR-4250
Suspected Offenses: insurance fraud and falsification of medical-admission records
Principal Persons: Jaeho Moon, Dayoung Lee, Seoyoon Choi, Taewon Kim, Minji Park
Relevant Organizations: Saebom Hospital, Hangyeol Insurance, Nuri Life
Principal Investigative Fact Pattern: patients who did not remain hospitalized were recorded as continuous inpatients, false treatment and nursing records were created, and portions of paid insurance benefits were returned to accounts controlled by the hospital
Principal Amount or Scale: KRW 628,000,000 in insurance payments
Principal Evidence Object: false admission records and insurance proceeds
Principal Locations: Saebom Hospital · Patient Workplaces · Insurance Companies

This section is structured as an integrated body of seized and collected digital, financial, communications, location, video-metadata, enterprise-system, and physical-evidence records. It is not intended as a narrative case summary; the relevant facts are intentionally dispersed across multiple evidence bundles.

# CASE 7
# Sungjin Logistics Customs-Evasion and Undervaluation Case

Case No.: 2026-CR-4286
Suspected Offenses: customs evasion and unlawful foreign-exchange transactions
Principal Persons: Taesik Yoo, Jungho Bae, Yujin Jang, Sehoon Jung, Hana Lee
Relevant Organizations: Sungjin Logistics, SJ Trading, Pacific Components
Principal Investigative Fact Pattern: commercial invoices submitted to customs reflected roughly one tenth of the actual transaction value, while the balance was remitted through affiliated-company accounts
Principal Amount or Scale: USD 418,000 actual value versus USD 42,000 declared value
Principal Evidence Object: dual invoices and high-value electronic components
Principal Locations: Southeast Customs Office · Sungjin Logistics Headquarters · Overseas Supplier

This section is structured as an integrated body of seized and collected digital, financial, communications, location, video-metadata, enterprise-system, and physical-evidence records. It is not intended as a narrative case summary; the relevant facts are intentionally dispersed across multiple evidence bundles.

# CASE 8
# MirTech Government R&D Grant Embezzlement and False-Invoice Case

Case No.: 2026-CR-4319
Suspected Offenses: grant fraud, embezzlement, and issuance of false tax invoices
Principal Persons: Sungho Jeon, Mira Oh, Changsoo Kim, Sungmin Jang, Haneul Yoo

This section is structured as an integrated body of seized and collected digital, financial, communications, location, video-metadata, enterprise-system, and physical-evidence records. # CASE 10
# Taesan Holdings Accounting Fraud, Fictitious Sales, and Loan-Fraud Case

Case No.: 2026-CR-4391
Suspected Offenses: accounting fraud, fraud, and breach of trust
Principal Persons: Jaesung Kwon, Jihoon Baek, Serin Lee, Hyunjoon Oh, Mina Choi
Relevant Organizations: Taesan Holdings, TS Distribution, Hansung Bank
Principal Investigative Fact Pattern: circular transactions with affiliates were recorded as genuine sales, inventory was overstated, and manipulated financial statements were used to obtain bank financing
Principal Amount or Scale: KRW 12,600,000,000 in fictitious sales and KRW 7,000,000,000 in loans
Principal Evidence Object: fictitious sales, inflated inventory, and loan proceeds
Principal Locations: Taesan Holdings Headquarters · Distribution Warehouse · Hansung Bank

This section is structured as an integrated body of seized and collected digital, financial, communications, location, video-metadata, enterprise-system, and physical-evidence records. It is not intended as a narrative case summary; the relevant facts are intentionally dispersed across multiple evidence bundles.

# CASE 12
# Metropolitan Road Project Inflated Material Pricing and Kickback Case

Case No.: 2026-CR-4458
Suspected Offenses: commercial bribery, kickbacks, and breach of trust
Principal Persons: Kyungsoo Park, Jinho Noh, Taekyung Yoon, Sehee Song, Moonho Lee
Relevant Organizations: Dongwoo Construction, Hangil Materials, Metropolitan Road Project Office
Principal Investigative Fact Pattern: construction materials were contracted at prices significantly above market levels and a portion of the excess was returned to project personnel in cash and gift certificates
Principal Amount or Scale: KRW 2,300,000,000 inflated contract and approximately KRW 230,000,000 in kickbacks
Principal Evidence Object: material payments and kickbacks
Principal Locations: Road Construction Site · Hangil Materials · Hotel Parking Garage

This section is structured as an integrated body of seized and collected digital, financial, communications, location, video-metadata, enterprise-system, and physical-evidence records. It is not intended as a narrative case summary; the relevant facts are intentionally dispersed across multiple evidence bundles.

# CASE 13
# Public-Sector IT Procurement Proposal Leak and Bid-Favoritism Case

Case No.: 2026-CR-4490
Suspected Offenses: disclosure of official secrets and interference with public procurement
Principal Persons: Wonseok Choi, Rahi Kim, Sungjin Moon, Eunbyeol Jo, Dohyun Lee
Relevant Organizations: National Data Agency, Sigma Systems, Onnuri IT
Principal Investigative Fact Pattern: nonpublic scoring criteria and portions of a competing bidder proposal were transmitted to a favored vendor before evaluation, which then revised its proposal to match the undisclosed criteria
Principal Amount or Scale: KRW 8,400,000,000 project value
Principal Evidence Object: confidential scoring sheet and competitor proposal
Principal Locations: National Data Agency · Sigma Systems · Evaluation Center

This section is structured as an integrated body of seized and collected digital, financial, communications, location, video-metadata, enterprise-system, and physical-evidence records. It is not intended as a narrative case summary; the relevant facts are intentionally dispersed across multiple evidence bundles.

# CASE 14

Case No.: 2026-CR-4526
Suspected Offenses: operation of illegal gambling facilities and concealment of criminal proceeds

# CASE 15
# Online Marketplace Seller-Settlement Misappropriation Case

Case No.: 2026-CR-4561
Suspected Offenses: large-scale embezzlement and fraud
Principal Persons: Sungjae Yoon, Hyorin Park, Dohyun Kim, Yuna Jeon, Jaehyung Seo
Relevant Organizations: MarketBridge, MB Pay, H Company
Principal Investigative Fact Pattern: seller proceeds that should have been held for settlement were used for affiliate operating expenses and the CEO personal debts, while later sales proceeds were used to cover earlier settlement obligations
Principal Amount or Scale: KRW 7,400,000,000 in unpaid seller settlements
Principal Evidence Object: seller settlement funds
Principal Locations: MarketBridge Headquarters · MB Pay Accounts · Affiliate Office

This section is structured as an integrated body of seized and collected digital, financial, communications, location, video-metadata, enterprise-system, and physical-evidence records. It is not intended as a narrative case summary; the relevant facts are intentionally dispersed across multiple evidence bundles.

# CASE 16
# Unauthorized Sale of Member Personal Data to Data Brokers Case

Case No.: 2026-CR-4597
Suspected Offenses: unlawful processing and disclosure of personal information
Principal Persons: Jaewon Kim, Yuri Hwang, Jungmin Noh, Chanhee Park, Seoa Lee
Relevant Organizations: ModuApp, DataCube, TargetAd

This section is structured as an integrated body of seized and collected digital, financial, communications, location, video-metadata, enterprise-system, and physical-evidence records. It is not intended as a narrative case summary; the relevant facts are intentionally dispersed across multiple evidence bundles.

# CASE 18
# Multi-Property Rental-Deposit Fraud and Nominee Ownership Case

Case No.: 2026-CR-4668
Suspected Offenses: fraud and unlawful nominee ownership of real property
Principal Persons: Junseok Lee, Boram Choi, Hyunwoo Kang, Soyoung Lim, Taejin Moon

- seized items + forensic examination results
- document version history + electronic approval logs
- account flow + related-party transaction records
- cloud activity + local-device file history
- multi-source chronological reconstruction across separate chunks

# CASE 19
# Nonprofit Subsidy Fraud Through False Payroll and Fictitious Services Case

Case No.: 2026-CR-4704
Suspected Offenses: subsidy fraud and false claims
Principal Persons: Kyungho Shin, Seojin Yoon, Minseok Kim, Chaewon Lim, Haneul Jung
Relevant Organizations: Green Future Association, Sharing Planning, Regional Welfare Foundation
Principal Investigative Fact Pattern: persons who did not actually work were reported as full-time employees and fictitious service contracts with related companies were used to divert public subsidy funds
Principal Amount or Scale: approximately KRW 310,000,000 of KRW 940,000,000 in subsidies
Principal Evidence Object: false payroll and fictitious service contracts
Principal Locations: Green Future Association · Sharing Planning · Regional Welfare Foundation

This section is structured as an integrated body of seized and collected digital, financial, communications, location, video-metadata, enterprise-system, and physical-evidence records. It is not intended as a narrative case summary; the relevant facts are intentionally dispersed across multiple evidence bundles.
